    - Reading. (i) P_full replaces E2's 'P0 first, then variants one by one'. (ii) Measuring each lever alone (the wishes of 2026-10-06 about 15:30 and 16:26, the offer of 19:07): seven single-lever trainings do not all fit at the high ends of the ledger of (12), but some fit when hours remain. P0, trained in the same job, measures what the levers add together; the optional single-lever DEV runs of (10h) measure V-37, V-MIX and V-TYPO alone first, then the wrappers and the packs, only from hours left after the required path, as information. (iii) The ladder of (9) can end without the levers or with them strongly weakened: steps 4 and 5 ship P0 or a mix of P0 with Z, models without any lever; step 6 ships a mix in which Z holds at least 0.7 of the weights and P_full at most 0.3; step 7 ships such a mix of P0, again without any lever; step 8 (F4) ships no public model at all (only Z is released). This goes against the requests of 18:42 and 18:45 and reverses the promise of 18:45 ('we ship only the version with all improvements that passed the check'). Steps 4, 5 and 7 also go against the reply of 20:28 that no improvement is removed ('its share is lowered and it is tried again, not removed'); that statement is replaced by the ladder of (9): the reruns with lowered shares (step 3) run only with hours left (at the planning highs of (12), 16.9 hours are left against the rerun's guard of 19.2), and steps 4, 5 and 7 can ship a model without levers. It is a decision taken under the delegation, with steps 6 and 7 added in response to the request of 18:42 (a finished and complete model of the project's own) and to the question of 21:08; it was approved at 21:09 ((13)). The claims made in the replies of 18:42 (a finished model in any case), 18:45 ('a finished model will exist for sure'), 19:15 ('a finished public model will exist in any case'), 20:28 ('guaranteed', 'it always passes the not worse check'), 20:49 and 21:08 ('the public model will always exist') are replaced by the rule of (9): a finished public model ships unless even the 0.05 mixes of steps 6 and 7 fail. That failure is practically impossible, because theta(a) approaches Z as a goes to 0, but it is not a guarantee: 'guaranteed', 'always passes' and the heading 'Guarantee' of 21:07 are withdrawn, and the mixing of 20:49 ('more and more strongly, until it passes') stops at a = 0.05. Before the look (J7) this sentence is given in the working chat: a finished public model ships unless even the 0.05 mixes fail. If there is an objection, J7 does not start until an amending entry is logged. (iv) A lever can stay out of P_full by a quality stop of its own data build or by a technical failure that a code-only fix and one rerun do not cure ((2)); this is wider than the promise of 18:56 ('something will not go in only when the check shows that it makes things worse'), and every such case is reported in the working chat before the P job. (v) Probe B12 of (3) may reverse the reply of 18:55: if it fires, P's honest part is data v2 and ML v2, the data of 1.1. The words of the reply of 21:01 ('if check B12 confirms that they do not spoil Lithuanian') are read by the rule of (3), as the summary of 20:49 stated it: pooled lt and pl with a margin of 0.010, so a loss on lt alone keeps data 1.2. (vi) The promise of 18:56 'not worse than 1.1 on clean text' is read as NW-P against Z (Z is 1.1, configs.B): not lower than Z by more than 0.010 on EN_DEC, LTPL, EN_unseen and LTPL_unseen, by more than 0.015 on lt_unseen, pl_unseen and de+sk_unseen, and by more than 0.020 on GUARD; (10a) and the model card say 'within 0.010 of Z on clean text', never 'not worse'. The words of the reply of 21:07 ('the not worse than 1.1 check will catch it ... it will not come out spoiled') are read the same way. (vii) The promise of 02:25 was written for variants chosen one by one; for P_full it becomes a report per noisy target ((10)), and P_full ships by NW-P alone ((9)). (viii) The promise of 19:08 to take the more accurate of two passing teachers is kept by the choice rule of (6). (ix) The promise of 18:56 that the finished model fits within 100 hours holds at the planning highs of (12), not at the jobs' ceilings; the hours guard of (12) then stops and asks in the working chat.

  - (2) Composition.
    - P0: E2's P0 with the checkpoint rule of (8) and the stripping scope of (5) (B's recipe on data pub of E1, caps (1), closed inventories (2), label texts (3), message noise (4) with AUG_STRIP_P_LTPL 0.30, carve-outs (5), adapter (6), no label-noise filter (7), E4's leakage removal (8)), with no lever; both changes are declared in (13). P0 is the measure of what the levers add and the public fallback.
    - P_full: P0's recipe plus (a) the D pack (4a), (b) V-37 (4b), (c) V-IND (4c), (d) the e-mail wrappers of V-E (4d), (e) V-TYPO (4e), (f) V-MIX (4f), (g) V-ADIA, the retrieval adapter trained also on stripped, wrapped and typo messages (4g), and (h) the KD term of V-TEACH only if the DEV teacher probe of (6) passes. Nothing else of E2 changes: the honest part (data 1.2 and ML 1.2 of E1, or data v2 and ML v2 if probe B12 of (3) fires), the caps, the closed inventories, the label texts outside V-MIX, the carve-outs, the absence of a label-noise filter and E4.
    - Stops. A lever's stops are of two kinds. A technical stop (fewer GPUs than the job needs, a crash, an out-of-memory error that the job's own halving does not absorb, a guard refusal, a missing output, a job ended by its time limit, or a fault in code such as one in Z's gate of (6)) is fixed in code, packages or staging only, never in a rule, share, setting, prompt or threshold, and the job runs once more after its own short note; only if that rerun also stops technically is the lever left out. A quality stop is a stop rule of a lever's own data (V-IND's yield and judge stops and the D-pack mask and merge stops, (4c)); V-IND has one registered second attempt ((4c)), and the wrapper floor of (4d) acts per language. A lever left out is recorded as not used and reported in the working chat before the P job, and P_full trains with the others. No stop rule is loosened, and no lever is rerun in any form other than those registered here.
  - (3) Honest part: probe B12.
    - Reason. Model D (B's recipe on data 1.2 plus the banking sources and the D pack) was below Z on matched DEV rows by 0.0131 on LTPL and by 0.0152 on clinc_dev (entry 2026-10-07 17:56). C12 and HN12, also trained on data 1.2, were below B on lt (HN12 lt 0.7326 against 0.7447, entry 2026-10-07 01:55; C12 lt 0.7293, `results/stage12_245443/stage12_decision_20261006T204627Z.json`, r12.quantities). The evidence is mixed: HN12's LTPL, 0.7446 against B's 0.7484, lies inside B's seed range 0.7428 to 0.7491 (entry 2026-10-07 11:12), and its pl, 0.7570, is above B's 0.7523 (entry 01:55); the sk control also fell (sk: B 0.7412, HN12 0.7355, C12 0.7247; same decision file, r12.quantities), so a large part of the one-pass arms' lt loss is not specific to lt (differences of the rounded values: HN12 lt -0.0121 and sk -0.0057; C12 lt -0.0154 and sk -0.0165). These runs differ from Z in more than the data, so the cause is not known. Data 1.2 differs from data v2 in more than rows: 56 of the 75,310 English base training rows were removed or changed (entry 2026-10-06 22:36); the synonym mask became mutual-only and in-label-set static negatives were added (entry 2026-10-06 21:47, items (1) and (4)); and the trainer's false-negative mask on data 1.2 adds the judge-synonym and directed data pairs (S3 of 2026-10-07 16:00, disclosure (2)). ML 1.2 has up to 12 lt and 12 pl rows per real training label against 6 in ML v2 (entry 2026-10-06 23:08; `results/step1ml_v2/step1_ml_manifest.json`, caps).
    - B12: B's recipe on data 1.2 and ML 1.2 alone (manifests 91993559ee1bac775f1d263ce340fb42034a213229170b1a1bf8ee4391941d3b and 9ece4703ab8b58e9bc9e491c3e1aa34884c50d41b832773516c21231196d39eb), exactly as JD trained model D: the v1 (a) cross-encoder recipe, the v1 epoch plan and sampler, 2 epochs, v1's periodic clinc_dev checkpoint selection over the whole run, the ONE-0 adapter with AD_STEPS 3,000, AUG_STRIP_P_LTPL 0.15 (the honest value), seed 0, w 60, k 5, tau null, v2 label texts; scored like D, with the honest comparison of D's record on the matched decision rows. One V100, `--time 03:00:00`, with the deadlines session 2.5 h, worker 2.2 h and latency skip 2.4 h in its builder mode (the final_dom deadlines of 4.5, 4.0 and 4.4 h with `--time 05:00:00`, `hpc/make_stage12_notebook.py` lines 9 and 71, do not fit), after the look (J7) and before J6b (data pub); DEV only, the guard refuses every test-named file; its sbatch line and notebook sha256 are logged in a short entry before it runs, and its result in its own entry.
    - Rule. If LTPL(B12) minus LTPL(Z) on the matched decision rows is below -0.010 (NW-P's margin; unrounded; a value equal to -0.010 does not fire), P's honest part (P0, P_full and every rerun) is data v2 and ML v2 (manifests a71da5c924517df9970add9e5adab99a3814b711cddd934f6919bc64b7d6ad19 and 559980c8c1ce5bbd78420f6f60b7c4eb36b63a0bf62a63534a7f66e445270be7) instead of data 1.2 and ML 1.2; everything else is unchanged, and the DEV stays the data 1.2 DEV with its ML 1.2 copies, so NW-P and the probes of (6) keep their rows. Otherwise the honest part is data 1.2 and ML 1.2, as E1. EN_DEC, clinc_dev and every pool are information. If B12 does not end FINAL, one identical rerun follows after its own note; if that also fails, the honest part is data 1.2 and ML 1.2, as E1 (disclosed). One seed carries run-to-run spread: B's LTPL ranged over 0.0064 across seeds 0, 1 and 2 (0.749117 minus 0.742756, dev_table of `results/stage12_seeds_245445/stage12_seeds_20261006T232026Z.json`, entry 2026-10-07 11:12). B12 is a DEV measurement only: never shipped, never evaluated on a registered test set, never a teacher, miner, noise model or generator, and it changes no honest model. The rule reads pooled LTPL only: a loss of up to 0.010 in lt and pl from data 1.2, and any loss on lt alone or on clinc_dev (which is in domain_seen_by_P and outside EN_DEC, so no NW-P group sees it), keeps data 1.2. This is delegated decision 4; it was approved at 21:09 ((13)).

B12 itself also needs a change: the FINAL dom path accepts only DOM_DATA_VARIANT 'dom' or 'dompack' (`tools/step1_stage12_build/src/cells_s12_part2.py` lines 176 and 177).
